### Blok: FAQ
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Digital Supply Chain architecture.
[P] What processes does the digital supply chain cover?
[S] In Detaysoft's solution structure, the Digital Supply Chain is addressed under four core process areas: planning, production, logistics, and supplier management.
[P] What solutions are available in the planning area?
[S] Planning includes Integrated Business Planning (IBP), Advanced Available-to-Promise (aATP), and Production Planning and Detailed Scheduling (PP/DS) solutions.
[P] What solutions can be used in production processes?
[S] Digital Manufacturing Cloud (DMC), Manufacturing Execution (ME), and Manufacturing Integration and Intelligence (MII) cover the production solution areas.
[P] What is available in the logistics solution area?
[S] Logistics includes Extended Warehouse Management (EWM), Transportation Management (TM), and Yard Management (YD).
[P] What does digitalization deliver in the supply chain?
[S] Digitalization helps speed up processes, create savings in cost and labor, enable real-time data tracking, and make supply chain performance more measurable.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about ERP and Digital Core.
[P] How can we determine the most suitable ERP solution for our company?
[S] Detaysoft experts run an analysis covering your company's current technology infrastructure, data volume, growth targets, and industry-specific needs, and design the architecture that will deliver the highest value.
[P] How long does the transition from our current ERP system to S/4HANA take, and will our operations be disrupted?
[S] It varies by company size and transition model. With our proven migration methodologies, the entire process is simulated, and you go live safely without disrupting operations.
[P] What advantages does Detaysoft's R&D Center bring to our company?
[S] Our Platinum status guarantees international project management of the highest quality, while our R&D Center guarantees innovative, company-specific solutions that go beyond standard ERP processes.
[P] Can our corporate data accumulated over the years and our company-specific developments be transferred to the new system?
[S] Yes. With our S/4HANA Conversion approach, your data, institutional memory, and custom developments (Z code) are analyzed, then renewed and migrated without data loss.
[P] What roadmap is followed before starting an ERP transformation project?
[S] We start with needs and concept analysis. Once the project scope, budget, and timeline are clear, we carry out the design, configuration, and go-live steps with confidence.

### Blok: FAQ
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about SAP SuccessFactors HCM solutions.
[P] What is human capital management?
[S] Human capital management is the set of applications and software an organization uses to hire, manage, and develop its employees.
[P] What is SAP SuccessFactors HCM?
[S] SAP SuccessFactors HCM is a connected suite of cloud applications supporting core HR and payroll, talent management, employee experience, HR analytics, and workforce planning.
[P] What does SAP SuccessFactors Employee Central provide?
[S] Employee Central provides a central HR information system for person and profile data, organizational structure, time and attendance tracking, transactions, and global benefits management.
[P] How is AI used in HR processes?
[S] AI can be used to automate core HR workflows, monitor data quality, support goal and performance processes, and generate actionable insights from workforce data.
[P] What decisions does Workforce Analytics support?
[S] Workforce Analytics supports monitoring workforce performance and making more data-driven decisions through standard HR metrics, data trends, and real-time insights.

### Blok: FAQ
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about Customer Experience solutions.
[P] What is customer experience (CX)?
[S] Customer experience refers to the overall experience formed across all of a customer's interactions with a brand or product. The journeys of both B2B and B2C customers can be addressed within this scope.
[P] Which business areas does SAP CX connect?
[S] The SAP CX approach aims to run marketing, commerce, sales, and service processes in connection with customer data and your existing SAP infrastructure.
[P] How do SAP CX solutions support the omnichannel customer experience?
[S] SAP CX and CRM solutions support engaging customers at different points of the experience journey and transforming the traditional CRM approach into an omnichannel structure.
[P] How is customer data used in CX processes?
[S] Evaluating ERP, customer experience, and other data sources together provides a more holistic data foundation for real-time insight and personalization in interactions across different channels.
[P] Where does e-commerce fit within the SAP CX structure?
[S] In SAP's CX approach, digital commerce is one of the core functions of the customer experience. SAP Commerce Cloud continues SAP's previous hybrid e-commerce capabilities within the SAP Customer Experience portfolio.

### Blok: FAQ
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the AI & Automation architecture.
[P] What is intelligent automation?
[S] Intelligent automation is the combination of traditional process automation with AI. This approach goes beyond processes that run only on predefined rules and supports automating more complex workflows.
[P] What are the core components of intelligent automation?
[S] Intelligent automation has three core components: AI, business process management, and robotic process automation (RPA).
[P] What is the difference between AI and traditional automation?
[S] Traditional automation relies on predefined rules, whereas AI-powered systems can analyze complex data, draw insight from business context, and adapt to new information.
[P] Which business areas can intelligent automation be used in?
[S] It can be used across different functions and departments, such as finance and accounting, supply chain logistics, and customer service.
[P] What should be considered when moving to intelligent automation?
[S] Beyond data quality, infrastructure, skills gaps, and technical debt, issues such as bias in AI, transparency, and accountability also need to be considered.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the logistics architecture.
[P] What processes does Supply Chain Logistics cover?
[S] It covers different operational areas such as warehouse management, transportation management, yard management, carrier collaboration, real-time visibility, and logistics management.
[P] What processes does warehouse management support?
[S] Warehouse management covers goods receipt, physical inventory, storage and replenishment planning, warehouse order optimization, packing inspection, process monitoring, and workforce demand planning.
[P] What stages does transportation management cover?
[S] Strategic freight management, order management, transportation planning, and transportation execution processes are supported. Integrated planning and AI-powered capabilities can also be used in transportation processes.
[P] What does real-time visibility provide?
[S] It supports real-time monitoring of shipments and order fulfillment, tracking of estimated arrival times, and automatic alerts for delays or deviations.
[P] What stages does delivery in logistics consist of?
[S] Receiving, storage, distribution, and analysis. This structure covers the process from the receipt of goods to delivery to the customer and performance evaluation.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the planning architecture.
[P] What is SAP IBP?
[S] SAP IBP brings sales and operations planning together with planning functions such as forecasting, demand, response, and supply, along with inventory management. It supports end-to-end planning with real-time analytics, simulation, and alerts.
[P] What planning capabilities does SAP IBP offer?
[S] The solution offers capabilities such as supply chain coordination, advanced machine learning, real-time monitoring and analytics, adaptation to demand changes, multi-echelon inventory optimization, and simulation of production, distribution, and supply plans.
[P] What does SAP aATP provide?
[S] SAP aATP supports checking product availability in order fulfillment processes. It enables real-time matching of supply and demand, evaluation of limited stock against customer priorities, and use of alternative product options.
[P] How are limited stocks managed with SAP aATP?
[S] With advanced availability checks, products with limited stock can be made visible and demands can be prioritized. The solution also supports matching constrained supply with customer-priority-based demands.
[P] What is SAP PP/DS?
[S] SAP PP/DS uses production data such as the production plan, bill of materials, capacity, working hours, and shifts to plan, sequence, and schedule production operations in detail.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Supplier Management architecture.
[P] What processes does Supplier Management cover?
[S] Supplier Management is addressed under five core solution areas: SAP Strategic Sourcing Management, SAP Supplier Risk Management, SAP Procurement and Invoicing, SAP Supplier Discovery, and SAP Workforce Management.
[P] What are the benefits of SAP Strategic Sourcing Management?
[S] It brings strategic procurement processes together on a shared platform, from spend data analysis to supplier and category management and from sourcing to contract management. It offers capabilities such as supplier segmentation, spend profile analysis, and total cost calculation.
[P] How can supplier risks be monitored?
[S] SAP Supplier Risk Management supports assessing supplier risks through continuous risk monitoring, risk detection, identification of risk causes, focused alerts, and third-party risk assessments.
[P] How can new suppliers be found?
[S] With SAP Supplier Discovery, you can publish procurement needs, compare supplier bids, and review detailed supplier profiles. You can benefit from a global network of millions of suppliers in more than 190 countries.
[P] How are procurement and invoicing processes managed?
[S] SAP Procurement and Invoicing supports automating manual tasks in processes from sourcing to payment. It offers capabilities such as budget controls, procurement policies, spend analysis, guided workflows, reporting, and Spot Buy.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the production architecture.
[P] What is SAP DMC?
[S] SAP Digital Manufacturing Cloud is a cloud-based manufacturing application that supports the connection between production and the enterprise. It enables real-time management and analysis of production processes and creates end-to-end visibility across different systems.
[P] Which systems does SAP DMC integrate with?
[S] SAP DMC can integrate with SAP S/4HANA and SAP ERP. This integration supports data flow across production, the supply chain, and other business processes.
[P] What does SAP ME provide?
[S] SAP Manufacturing Execution supports the operational management of production processes and the execution of production orders. It provides end-to-end operational visibility with digital management, supervision, production planning control, and automation capabilities.
[P] Does SAP ME support product tracking?
[S] Yes. It is listed as one of the core capabilities SAP ME offers. Real-time data also supports closer tracking of production processes and products.
[P] What is SAP MII used for?
[S] SAP MII links workforce, raw material, method, and machine elements with the supply chain. By creating data flow between disparate systems, it supports the integrated management of production processes.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about SAP Cloud ERP Private migration and architecture.
[P] What is SAP Cloud ERP Private?
[S] SAP Cloud ERP Private is a custom-configurable cloud ERP solution that moves existing on-premises SAP ERP investments to the cloud while adapting to a business's unique processes and transformation needs.
[P] Who is SAP Cloud ERP Private suitable for?
[S] It suits businesses that want to protect their existing SAP ERP investment, maintain their unique processes, and move to the cloud at their own pace. The solution offers comprehensive ERP capabilities together with flexible deployment and customization options.
[P] Does SAP Cloud ERP Private support existing customizations?
[S] The solution allows business-specific processes to be adapted and extended with cloud-compatible extensions. The clean core approach helps preserve differentiation while reducing technical debt and making innovations easier to access.
[P] How can SAP Cloud ERP Private be deployed?
[S] The solution can be deployed on hyperscaler infrastructure, in a private data center, or in a sovereign cloud. Businesses can choose the deployment option that fits their security, data residency, and operational requirements.
[P] How does SAP Cloud ERP Private support update and integration needs?
[S] The solution enables integration with existing systems and lets new capabilities be rolled out according to the organization's business priorities. The update approach can also be planned with the existing operating structure and technical requirements in mind.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Accounts Payable and Receivable architecture.
[P] What is the SAP Accounts Payable and Receivable solution?
[S] The SAP Accounts Payable and Receivable solution is a financial management structure that supports automating accounts payable and accounts receivable processes. It helps increase the speed and accuracy of processes, assess credit risk, and improve payment visibility.
[P] Which financial processes does the SAP Accounts Payable and Receivable solution support?
[S] The solution supports assessing customer credit risk, simplifying invoicing systems, improving invoice management, monitoring receivables and payments, and managing payment plans.
[P] How does accounts payable and receivable management contribute to cash flow?
[S] Tighter control over invoice management and real-time payment monitoring help increase cash flow and payment visibility. Identifying risky accounts earlier also helps prioritize collection processes.
[P] Can sales and receivables processes be managed together?
[S] SAP revenue management solutions link processes from sales through accounts receivable. They support managing pricing, quoting, invoicing, and revenue processes from a shared data source.
[P] Which deployment model can the accounts payable and receivable solution be used with?
[S] A server-based deployment option is specified for the SAP S/4HANA Finance receivables management solution.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Financial Planning and Analysis architecture.
[P] What is FP&A software?
[S] FP&A software uses financial and operational data to model, monitor, and evaluate results. It supports budgeting, planning, forecasting, and performance analysis processes.
[P] What processes does SAP Financial Planning and Analysis support?
[S] The solution supports budgeting, forecasting, scenario modeling, performance analysis, strategic planning, and comparing plans with actual results.
[P] How does SAP Analytics Cloud contribute to financial planning?
[S] SAP Analytics Cloud combines planning, business intelligence, and predictive capabilities in one solution. It supports finance teams in accessing data centrally, running real-time analyses, and collaborating in planning processes.
[P] How is AI used in financial planning?
[S] AI supports tasks such as forecasting, variance analysis, scenario simulation, model creation, and plan updates. This lets finance teams move from periodic planning to a more continuous, insight-driven working model.
[P] How are planning results tracked?
[S] By continuously comparing assumptions with actual results, variances and risks can be identified. The insights gained can be used to update plans and, when necessary, correct course.

[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Real Estate Management architecture.
[P] What processes does real estate management cover?
[S] Real estate management covers tracking portfolio, contract, lease, cost, space utilization, and facility information together. This structure helps manage real estate operations in a more orderly and holistic way.
[P] How can the real estate portfolio and contracts be tracked more effectively?
[S] Centralizing portfolio and contract information makes it easier to evaluate lease terms, costs, and usage data together. This gives faster access to up-to-date information on real estate assets.
[P] How is lease accounting integrated with financial processes?
[S] Integrating lease accounting with financial systems supports transferring the financial data arising from lease contracts into the relevant accounting processes. This allows lease information and financial records to be handled in a more consistent structure.
[P] How can real estate costs and space utilization be monitored?
[S] Evaluating costs by facility, space, and portfolio together with usage data helps you see more clearly how resources are being used. With the analytics structure, cost and usage information can be tracked in a shared view.
[P] What does ERP integration provide for real estate management?
[S] ERP integration supports connecting data from real estate processes with finance, accounting, and other enterprise processes. This allows portfolio, contract, and cost information to be evaluated more holistically within the organization's existing system landscape.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Treasury Management architecture.
[P] What is treasury management and which financial processes does it cover?
[S] Treasury management covers tracking cash position, liquidity, bank transactions, financial risks, investments, and borrowing processes together. The aim is to evaluate financial data in a timely way and run treasury operations with more control.
[P] How does the solution increase cash visibility?
[S] Real-time data and forecasting capabilities help provide a more holistic view of current liquidity and future cash needs.
[P] How is financial risk management supported?
[S] The solution supports monitoring price, currency, counterparty credit, and system risks. Risk positions can be assessed on up-to-date data to develop hedging and financing strategies.
[P] Can bank transactions be automated within treasury management?
[S] Handling bank transactions in a central, integrated structure can help reduce manual workload and track financial movements in a more orderly way. This makes the information flow between bank data and treasury operations more effective.
[P] Why are investment and debt management part of treasury processes?
[S] Evaluating investments and debt together enables a more holistic view of the organization's cash needs, financing structure, and use of resources. This approach helps treasury teams make better-informed decisions based on the current financial position.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Accounting and Financial Close architecture.
[P] What is the SAP accounting and financial close solution?
[S] SAP accounting and financial close solutions are an integrated solution structure for automating and organizing the record-to-report process. They help manage financial accounting transactions, speed up period-end closes, and support accurate reporting.
[P] How does the solution speed up the financial close?
[S] Through continuous processing of detailed data, automated postings and reconciliations, close templates, and connected workflows, it helps reduce manual tasks. Close progress can be monitored in real time, and issues can be addressed earlier
[P] How does SAP S/4HANA Finance contribute to close processes?
[S] SAP S/4HANA Finance unites financial and management data in a shared ledger structure. With real-time accounting and reporting, it helps you reach up-to-date business insights without waiting for the month-end close.
[P] Does the solution support data from different ERP systems?
[S] Central finance capabilities support connecting financial data from SAP ERP and third-party ERP sources. This makes it possible to create a unified financial view of transaction details coming from different systems.
[P] Why is close automation important?
[S] Automation reduces manual workload and the risk of errors while allowing significant variances to be seen earlier.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Travel and Expense Management architecture.
[P] What is SAP Concur?
[S] SAP Concur is a portfolio of cloud-based solutions that automate and connect travel, expense, and invoice management processes. It simplifies spend management and helps organizations run their processes more efficiently and with greater control.
[P] What processes does SAP Concur support?
[S] It supports processes such as business travel booking, expense reporting, receipt capture, policy checks, invoice management, approval, reimbursement, and spend analysis.
[P] What does automating expense management provide?
[S] Automation helps reduce manual workload and the risk of errors. It lets organizations monitor spending more closely, support policy compliance, and base their decisions on more accurate data
[P] Can SAP Concur integrate with SAP ERP systems?
[S] SAP Concur solutions can integrate with SAP ERP, SAP S/4HANA, and SAP Cloud ERP. This integration helps connect travel and expense data with financial processes and provides up-to-date insights into spending.
[P] Can employees manage expense reports from mobile devices?
[S] Yes. Employees can capture receipts, create and submit expense reports, and review their reports while traveling through the mobile app. Mobile access also helps speed up approvals and reimbursements.

[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Cybersecurity, Governance, Risk and Compliance architecture.
[P] What is SAP GRC?
[S] SAP GRC is an integrated, modular suite of solutions that helps align business objectives, manage risks, and ensure compliance with regulations and internal policies.
[P] Which core areas does SAP GRC cover?
[S] The solution covers four core areas: enterprise risk and compliance, identity and access governance, cybersecurity and data protection, and international trade management.
[P] Which deployment options can SAP GRC be used with?
[S] Businesses can evaluate the right model based on their existing systems and security requirements.
[P] Is SAP GRC integrated with SAP S/4HANA?
[S] SAP GRC solutions can work integrated with SAP S/4HANA and are available through additional licensing.
[P] How does SAP GRC support cybersecurity?
[S] The cybersecurity and data protection solutions within SAP GRC support the secure use of applications. They help monitor threats and enable risk, audit, access, and data protection processes to be managed in a shared framework.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Compensation architecture.
[P] What is SAP SuccessFactors Compensation?
[S] SAP SuccessFactors Compensation is a compensation management solution that enables salary, bonus, incentive, and benefits processes to be managed on a central platform. It supports linking pay decisions to performance and business goals.
[P] How is compensation planning managed?
[S] Compensation plans can be created and managed on a central platform. Configurable views and real-time budget insights support a more transparent and controlled planning process.
[P] What tools can be used in compensation design?
[S] It offers templates based on guidelines and budget modeling, bonus forecasting, and compensation practices. With these tools, different pay models can be created according to performance and competencies.
[P] Can employees access pay and benefits information from mobile devices?
[S] Yes. Employees can view their salary details, benefits, and past payment information from mobile devices. Managers can also access compensation and budget planning processes through mobile.
[P] Can Compensation integrate with other systems?
[S] Yes. SAP SuccessFactors can work integrated with SAP S/4HANA, Joule, other SAP solutions, and non-SAP systems.

[S] Managers and employees can set individual and team goals together. AI-powered suggestions help create goals that are clearer and measurable.
[P] How are feedback processes managed?
[S] Employees and managers can share real-time feedback through the system. With 360-degree feedback support, evaluations from different people can be included in the same performance process.
[P] How is AI used in performance management?
[S] AI offers suggestions and insights in areas such as goal creation, performance reviews, feedback and employee development. In SAP's current solution, AI-powered goal management and the Performance Preparation Agent are among the highlighted capabilities.
[P] Is mobile access available?
[S] Yes. Employees and managers can access goals, feedback and performance reviews through the mobile app, and carry out actions such as goal setting and giving feedback from their mobile devices.

[P] What is SAP SuccessFactors Time Tracking?
[S] SAP SuccessFactors Time Tracking is a solution that enables time and attendance data to be recorded digitally, monitored in real time and managed in connection with payroll processes.
[P] Can Time Tracking be used on mobile devices?
[S] Yes. Employees can clock in and out from mobile devices based on location. Managers can also review, approve and analyze time records on mobile screens.
[P] Can it be integrated with non-SAP systems?
[S] Yes. The solution supports data sharing with external time clocks and shift planning software. This helps adapt Time Tracking to the existing company infrastructure.
[P] How is legal compliance supported?
[S] Localized time management scenarios support managing country-specific rules, working hours and overtime regulations in the system.
[P] How are workforce costs tracked?
[S] Automated alerts can be set up based on thresholds defined for lateness and overtime. This supports cost control by surfacing exceptions in working hours earlier.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the SAP for Universities architecture.
[P] What does SAP HCM for Universities provide?
[S] SAP HCM supports handling the HR processes of academic and administrative staff at universities within a common framework. Managing personnel data centrally helps processes run more consistently and keeps HR teams working with up-to-date information.
[P] Can academic and administrative staff processes be managed in one system?
[S] Processes for academic and administrative staff with different roles, units and working structures can be brought together on a common HR infrastructure. This way, data on different employee groups can be tracked through a central framework and a more holistic approach can be built across the institution.
[P] How does SAP HCM support workforce planning at universities?
[S] Evaluating up-to-date employee and organizational data together enables better analysis of the current workforce structure. This data can be used in assessing staffing needs, resource planning and future human resources decisions.
[P] Why is HR analytics important at universities?
[S] HR analytics allows employee and organizational data to be used not only for record-keeping but also in planning and decision-making. Analyzing up-to-date data on academic and administrative staff offers a more comprehensive view of the institution's human resources structure.
[P] How can data integrity be ensured in campus-wide HR processes?
[S] Collecting HR data in a common system helps make the information used across different units more consistent and accessible. A central data structure supports running employee information, the organizational structure and related HR processes on the same data foundation.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Work Zone architecture.
[P] What is SAP SuccessFactors Work Zone?
[S] SAP SuccessFactors Work Zone is a solution that brings business applications, content and processes together in a single personalized digital workspace. It supports employees in finding information, connecting with teams and carrying out daily tasks more easily.
[P] Does Work Zone work only with SAP applications?
[S] No. In addition to SAP applications, Work Zone can integrate with non-SAP systems. It supports presenting content and tasks from different platforms in a shared user experience.
[P] How does Work Zone support internal communication?
[S] It enables two-way communication from manager to employee and among employees through notifications, forums, comments and feedback areas. This framework supports employee engagement and knowledge sharing.
[P] How does Work Zone contribute to orientation processes?
[S] Through communities, templates and central content, it helps new employees or team members changing roles reach the information they need faster.
[P] Does Work Zone reduce the workload of IT teams?
[S] Being able to manage content and processes without IT support helps reduce IT dependency in daily update and editing work.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the SAP CX architecture.
[P] What is SAP CX?
[S] SAP CX is a solution portfolio that helps businesses manage the customer experience across different touchpoints. It supports responding to customer expectations, building new channels and managing the customer experience more holistically.
[P] Which solution areas does SAP CX cover?
[S] SAP CX includes E-Commerce, Emarsys, SAP Sales Cloud, SAP Service Cloud and Customer Data Solutions. This framework covers commerce, marketing, sales, service and customer data processes.
[P] How does SAP CX support a personalized customer experience?
[S] Data from different customer profiles can be used in marketing and customer interactions. With insight-driven offers and personalized solutions, experiences at different stages of the customer journey can be shaped around each customer's needs.
[P] Can SAP CX integrate with existing systems?
[S] Yes. SAP CX solutions are designed to be applied to and integrated with existing systems. Thanks to the modular structure, the solution scope can be expanded as needs evolve.
[P] Does SAP CX provide real-time analytics?
[S] SAP CX supports analyzing data in real time and using real-time, predictive analytics. This approach helps sales and service teams act on up-to-date information.

[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about Ready4 Asset Management (R4AM).
[P] What is Ready4 Asset Management (R4AM)?
[S] R4AM is a platform designed to digitalize and automate asset maintenance and field service processes. It covers different stages, from work requests to maintenance planning and field operations.
[P] Can R4AM work integrated with SAP systems?
[S] Yes. R4AM establishes a two-way connection with SAP systems to support running maintenance data and work processes in an integrated way.
[P] How are maintenance requests assessed?
[S] Notifications from the field are recorded, validated and prioritized centrally. Requests are assessed for urgency and converted into the appropriate work orders.
[P] How are maintenance planning and scheduling done?
[S] Historical maintenance data, workforce capacity, equipment and spare parts needs are assessed. Maintenance activities and resource use are planned with graphical schedules.
[P] Can field teams track maintenance processes on mobile?
[S] R4AM gives field technicians mobile access to work orders and checklists. With online and offline working options, it supports carrying out field activities.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about Document Management.
[P] What is OpenText Document Access (ADA)?
[S] OpenText Document Access (ADA) is a solution that provides single-point access to documents held in different systems through SAP processes and applications. It supports central document access and archiving.
[P] Which systems' documents does Document Access cover?
[S] It covers documents related to legacy software, hosting systems, company-specific applications and SAP systems. It makes content from different sources centrally accessible.
[P] Does OpenText Document Access work integrated with SAP?
[S] Yes. It supports viewing documents related to SAP ERP, SAP CRM and other SAP modules through business processes.
[P] What archiving features does Document Access offer?
[S] It supports viewing archived documents in a folder view, bulk document scanning and local scanning for special files. Email content can also be stored in the central archive.
[P] What benefits does Document Access offer businesses?
[S] It simplifies document access, increases transparency in business processes and reduces paper use. Through data archiving and decommissioning legacy systems, it supports lowering the total cost of ownership.

[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about Move2AD.
[P] What is Move2AD?
[S] Move2AD is an integration solution that automates personnel transactions between SAP HR and Microsoft Active Directory. It handles the creation, update and deactivation of user accounts.
[P] Which systems does Move2AD work with?
[S] Move2AD is designed for organizations that use SAP HR and Microsoft Active Directory. Personnel data is transferred from SAP HR to Active Directory.
[P] How does Move2AD simplify hiring processes?
[S] It automatically creates the Active Directory account of an employee hired in SAP HR. It supports mapping the relevant personnel fields between the two systems.
[P] Are organization and position changes updated automatically?
[S] Yes. Position, role and organization changes in SAP HR are automatically reflected in Active Directory user information.
[P] What happens to the Active Directory account of an employee who leaves?
[S] When an exit is processed in SAP HR, the corresponding Active Directory user account is automatically deactivated.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about Correspondence Management.
[P] What is OpenText Correspondence Management?
[S] OpenText Correspondence Management is a solution that enables internal and external official correspondence to be created, tracked, approved and archived electronically.
[P] Does OpenText Correspondence Management support electronic signatures?
[S] Yes. The solution offers electronic and mobile signature support compliant with Law No. 5070, along with signature verification features.
[P] Can OpenText Correspondence Management work integrated with SAP?
[S] Yes. It supports integration with SAP ERP and other systems. It allows master data such as vendors, customers and projects to be used in correspondence processes.
[P] How is official correspondence archived?
[S] Completed correspondence is automatically archived together with the related documents and content. Access to documents is provided through user permissions and advanced search features.
[P] Can the status of incoming and outgoing documents be tracked?
[S] Yes. The current status of correspondence can be viewed through the document verification interface. Task emails can be used to notify users of pending actions and daily document lists.

[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about SAP Real Estate Management.
[P] What is SAP Real Estate Management (SAP RE-FX)?
[S] SAP RE-FX is a solution that supports end-to-end management of the real estate portfolio from a single hub. It aims to reduce manual processes and run the related operations in an integrated way.
[P] Which processes does SAP RE-FX cover?
[S] It covers investment management, space management, lease contract management and maintenance and repair management processes. It also supports tracking of information such as rent increase rates, payment dates and property ownership structure.
[P] Where is it used in the banking sector?
[S] It is used in branch renovation work, lease contract tracking, cost tracking and space management processes involving square-meter and volume data. It also supports central monitoring of areas such as penalty clauses, periodic increases and payment terms.
[P] What does it provide in retail, REIT and shopping mall structures?
[S] It enables sublease invoicing and contracts, store renewals, maintenance and repair operations, lease tracking and overall cost tracking to be managed in one place.
[P] How does SAP RE-FX support reporting and cost evaluation?
[S] By working in integration with modules such as SAP FI and CO, it helps produce error-free reports from accurate data. It supports cost evaluation and more controlled execution of recurring processes.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about SAP EWM.
[P] What is SAP Extended Warehouse Management (EWM)?
[S] SAP EWM is a solution that enables the management of high-volume warehouse operations and stock movements. It covers goods receipt, storage, order picking and shipping.
[P] Which warehouse processes does SAP EWM support?
[S] It supports activities such as goods receipt, inventory control, physical inventory, picking, packing, shipping and labor planning. It also covers cross-docking and material flow control.
[P] How does SAP EWM simplify inventory tracking?
[S] It enables tracking of stock movements, physical inventory and cycle counts. It supports real-time visibility into warehouse activities.
[P] Can SAP EWM be used in the cloud?
[S] Yes. SAP EWM offers application options that can be used on-premise or in the cloud.
[P] Does SAP EWM support reporting and resource planning?
[S] Yes. It supports shift and resource planning with advanced labor tools. With SAP Analytics Cloud, it allows warehouse activities to be analyzed and reported.

[P] Find answers to your questions about SOA People Academy.
[P] In which areas does SOA People Academy offer training?
[S] The program covers SAP finance, logistics, analytics, human resources and software development. FI, CO, TRM, SD, MM, EWM, PP, PM, QM, BW, BO, BPC, SAC, HCM, ABAP and Fiori are among the training topics.
[P] How long does the Academy training last and how is it delivered?
[S] The announced program is planned to last four weeks. The training is designed to be delivered free of charge, live and online via Microsoft Teams.
[P] Is a certificate awarded at the end of the Academy?
[S] Participants receive a certificate of attendance, and those who pass the exam at the end of the program receive a certificate of achievement. Successful participants are also offered the opportunity to work with the team and, if hired, additional training opportunities.
[P] Are applications for SOA People Academy still open?
[S] The application period for this term has expired. The current application dates, requirements and capacity for a new term will be announced.

[P] Find answers to your questions about SOA People Dealer Portal.
[P] What is SOA People Dealer Portal?
[S] SOA People Dealer Portal is a solution that helps you bring ordering, shipping, authorization and commercial rule management for your dealer network onto a single platform.
[P] Can the Dealer Portal work integrated with SAP?
[S] Yes. The solution works in an SAP-integrated structure and supports managing customer data, product information, payment terms and order processes together.
[P] Can dealer-specific authorizations be set up?
[S] Yes. The pages dealer users can see and the actions they can perform can be customized according to needs.
[P] Can order and shipping processes be tracked through the portal?
[S] Yes. Created orders, order movements and shipping processes can be monitored through the portal.
[P] Can rules such as price, credit limit and order quantity be defined?
[S] Yes. Commercial rules such as dealer-specific price lists, credit limits and minimum and maximum order quantities can be defined.

[S] It makes it easier to keep existing SAP systems up to date and to carry out development work in line with changing needs. In this way, it contributes to getting more value from the SAP infrastructure.

[P] What is Solution Project?
[S] Solution Project is an industry solution that aims to handle project activities in the construction and contracting sector more holistically. It focuses on accounting, purchasing, budget and construction site processes.
[P] Which industries is Solution Project designed for?
[S] It focuses on the needs of the construction, contracting and engineering sectors. It particularly addresses the operational challenges encountered in long-term, high-budget projects.

[S] It supports evaluating budget information held in different applications together. In this way, it focuses on reducing the difficulties experienced in central budget tracking.
[P] Which problems does Solution Project focus on?
[S] It focuses on incorrect material purchases, fragmented financial records and inefficiencies that cause projects to run longer. It aims to reduce disconnects between business processes.

[P] What is OpenText Vendor Invoice Management (VIM)?
[S] OpenText VIM is an SAP-integrated solution that automates the acquisition, routing, approval and payment of vendor invoices. It simplifies invoice management by reducing manual work.
[P] Does OpenText VIM work integrated with SAP?
[S] Yes. By working in integration with SAP applications, OpenText VIM supports automatic transfer of invoice data and execution of the related workflows.

[P] How does OpenText VIM contribute to financial processes?
[S] It supports access to up-to-date vendor and payment account information and makes financial reporting easier. With automated workflows, it helps shorten the payment cycle.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about R4AM.
[P] What is Ready4 Asset Management (R4AM)?
[S] R4AM is a platform that digitizes asset maintenance and field service processes in SAP systems. It covers different stages, from work requests to maintenance planning and field activities.
[P] How does R4AM work with SAP systems?
[S] By establishing a bidirectional connection with SAP systems, it supports integrated use of maintenance data. It enables central monitoring of enterprise information.
[P] How are maintenance requests assessed?
[S] Notifications from the field are recorded, verified and prioritized by urgency. Suitable requests are converted into work orders and included in maintenance preparations.
[P] Can field teams use the platform offline?
[S] Yes. R4AM supports technicians accessing work orders and related information from mobile devices, online or offline.
[P] How is the completion of maintenance work verified?
[S] Completion is assessed through work orders, quality checks and formal verification processes. Through approval transactions, the completion of maintenance activities can be recorded.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Innovations architecture.
[P] Which business needs do SAP’s AI innovations focus on?
[S] SAP positions its innovations around turning fixed processes into more responsive workflows, making supply chains more proactive and moving generic interactions toward more personalized experiences. Rather than limiting AI to a single use case, this approach aims to spread it across the business’s different processes.
[P] How is AI used in workflow automation?
[S] Context-aware Joule Agents automate tasks in specific business processes, while Joule Assistants can coordinate different agents based on role and process context. This way, AI can be used not only for individual tasks but also in more complex, multi-step workflows.
[P] Are AI innovations designed only for end users?
[S] No. SAP’s innovations also cover developer teams. SAP Domain Models support use cases such as code generation, modernization and querying enterprise data in natural language, while SAP ABAP-1 helps developers get faster access to up-to-date ABAP Cloud knowledge and code understanding.
[P] How can I decide which AI innovation is right for my business?
[S] First, identify the business need you want to solve. Agents and assistants can be used to run complex workflows, SAP Knowledge Graph to connect enterprise data with AI, Generative AI Hub to manage different models and SAP Document AI tools for document processes.
[P] Can different AI models be managed in the same structure?
[S] Generative AI Hub supports building, testing and deploying solutions that use models from different providers. With central management, flexible deployment, security and governance capabilities, businesses can evaluate different models within a more controlled structure.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about Use Cases.
[P] What types of business processes are AI agents suited to?
[S] Repetitive, error-prone, time-consuming and data-intensive processes are especially good starting points. Cross-functional, complex or compliance-critical workflows can also be supported by AI agents.
[P] How should the first use case be chosen?
[S] You can start with a manageable and measurable use case. Once a specific process such as invoice processing or dispute resolution is selected, clear success indicators such as time savings or fewer errors can be defined.
[P] Why is data readiness important for AI agents?
[S] Enterprise AI agents rely on high-quality, harmonized data. For this reason, real-time data access, integration capabilities and data governance standards should be assessed before implementation.
[P] Which departments can use AI agents?
[S] Separate use cases can be applied for finance and accounting, supply chain, human resources, manufacturing, marketing and sales, IT and customer support.
[P] Which teams should be involved in the implementation process?
[S] AI integration requires cross-functional work. Business leaders, IT teams and end users should be involved in the process, and change management, data privacy and business impact should be assessed throughout implementation.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the SAP Business AI architecture.
[P] What is SAP Business AI?
[S] SAP Business AI is SAP’s approach to using AI in connection with SAP business processes and enterprise data. It aims to support processes and decisions by bringing together business context, data and AI models.
[P] What is the key difference of SAP Business AI?
[S] In SAP’s approach, AI works in the context of real business processes and enterprise data. This makes it possible to build AI applications that go beyond general-purpose models and take the business’s processes and data into account.
[P] Which systems can SAP Business AI work with?
[S] The Business AI experience is not limited to SAP systems and can work in connection with non-SAP systems as well. This makes it possible to build more unified workflows across different applications.
[P] What approach does SAP Business AI take to safe use?
[S] SAP treats security, governance, ethical safeguards and human oversight as core components of enterprise AI use.
[P] How are Joule and SAP Business AI related?
[S] Joule is one of the user-facing layers of SAP’s AI experience. It brings together business-context insights, assistants and AI agents in the workflow, supporting actions toward users’ goals.

### Blok: FAQ / 1 /
[H1] Frequently Asked Questions
[P] Find answers to your questions about the Generative AI architecture.
[P] How can generative AI be used in business processes?
[S] Generative AI can be used in areas such as automating workflows, drawing knowledge from enterprise data, document processing, generating predictive insights and application development.
[P] What is the difference between Joule Agents and Joule Assistants?
[S] Joule Agents are AI agents specialized in specific business processes. Joule Assistants, in turn, coordinate these agents using role and process context and support the execution of more complex workflows.
[P] What does SAP Generative AI Hub provide?
[S] It supports building, testing and deploying AI solutions using models from different providers. It also offers central management, security, governance and flexible deployment capabilities.
[P] What is SAP Knowledge Graph for?
[S] SAP Knowledge Graph grounds AI in enterprise data. It aims to deliver reliable information across applications, make data discovery easier and support business decisions.
[P] What is SAP Document AI used for?
[S] SAP Document AI enables AI-powered processing of structured and unstructured data in different documents and supports automating document-driven workflows.
